Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in magnetometry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 774 and the invariant I(A,B) = 354 mod 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the magnetometry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 774 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 354. QED.
